- **theurian-adversarial-review**: Adversarial review for Theurian. One of the three mandatory pre-Ready reviews. Its job is to break the change and to disprove the code's own claims by running it, not by reading it. ([theurian/theurian](https://github.com/theurian/theurian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/theurian-adversarial-review.md) · ★6)
- **theurian-ci**: CI, packaging, and release specialist for Theurian. Use when a GitHub Actions job fails, when packaging or SBOM changes, or when a PR is blocked by a check. Knows the failures this repository has already hit and why. ([theurian/theurian](https://github.com/theurian/theurian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/theurian-ci.md) · ★6)
- **theurian-code-review**: Code review for Theurian. One of the three mandatory pre-Ready reviews (with theurian-security-review and theurian-adversarial-review). Reviews correctness and maintainability against this project's layering rules and ADRs. ([theurian/theurian](https://github.com/theurian/theurian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/theurian-code-review.md) · ★6)
- **theurian-docs**: Documentation specialist for Theurian. Use when a change needs README, CHANGELOG, ADR, or docs/ updates — especially breaking changes, new ADRs, and keeping ADR compliance sections honest. ([theurian/theurian](https://github.com/theurian/theurian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/theurian-docs.md) · ★6)
- **theurian-mcp**: MCP protocol specialist for Theurian. Use when adding or changing MCP tools, the Streamable HTTP daemon, tool schemas, or the safety labelling on results. Knows the SDK 2.0 API and the traps this project has already hit. ([theurian/theurian](https://github.com/theurian/theurian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/theurian-mcp.md) · ★6)
- **theurian-python**: Python implementation specialist for Theurian Core. Use for writing or refactoring Python in packages/theurian-core — domain models, application services, adapters, and the CLI. Knows this project's layering rules, typing bar, and idioms. ([theurian/theurian](https://github.com/theurian/theurian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/theurian-python.md) · ★6)
- **theurian-security-review**: Security review for Theurian. One of the three mandatory pre-Ready reviews. Checks changes against this project's SEC-* requirements and threat model, including MCP prompt-injection surfaces. ([theurian/theurian](https://github.com/theurian/theurian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/theurian-security-review.md) · ★6)
- **theurian-tests**: Test specialist for Theurian. Use to write tests for new behaviour, to close coverage gaps, and to check that existing tests can actually fail. Enforces the mutation discipline this project relies on. ([theurian/theurian](https://github.com/theurian/theurian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/theurian-tests.md) · ★6)
- **thevai-agent**: Collect a complete, approved brief from a person over WhatsApp, then build, QA, and deliver their deliverables. Loads the right skills for the build (pptx, docx, pdf, xlsx, dataviz), gathers requirements through a warm short-message conversation, gates on the person's explicit approval, builds, renders for visual QA, delivers on WhatsApp, and pushes to GitHub. Use when Aswath says "ask X what they want and do it", "collect requirements and build", or "thevai &lt;name&gt;". ([aswathas/aswath-skills](https://github.com/aswathas/aswath-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/workflow/aswath-thevai/agents/thevai-agent.md))
- **Thiago Nascimento**: 1. Antes de produzir, ler `squads/administrativo/reunioes/alinhamento/reuniao-alinhamento/_memory/design-tokens.md` para cores, fontes e espaçamentos. - **thiago_finch**: &gt; **Business Strategy & Marketing Architect** | Funnel-First Thinking | Core + lazy-loaded knowledge ([rodrigo-altissimo/qiartificial](https://github.com/rodrigo-altissimo/qiartificial/blob/HEAD/squads/squad-creator-pro/agents/thiago_finch.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **thiel**: Evaluates ideas as Peter Thiel -- zero-to-one thinking, monopoly potential, contrarian truths, definite optimism ([boomhaus/skills](https://github.com/boomhaus/skills/blob/HEAD/boom-biz/agents/thiel.md))
- **thiel**: Contrarian framing editor applying Peter Thiel's principles from 'Zero to One.' Tests whether the piece contains a genuine contrarian insight, whether the writer is thinking for themselves, and whether the argument has the courage of a definite vision. ([mvoitko/dotfiles](https://github.com/mvoitko/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/plugins/writing-council/agents/thiel.md))
- **thiel-judge**: あなたは事業分析パイプラインの定石適用担当である。**新規の検索はしない。** 渡された材料（P1事実・P2構造変化・P3前例カルテ・HITL注入情報）と references/ の2ファイル（thiel-canon.md / thiel-current-bets.md）だけで判定する。前置き不要。 ([Mitachi-H/thiel-lens](https://github.com/Mitachi-H/thiel-lens/blob/HEAD/agents/thiel-judge.md) · ★1)
- **thiet-ke-nghien-cuu**: Thiết kế nghiên cứu y khoa TRƯỚC khi có dữ liệu — chọn thiết kế phù hợp, kiểm soát sai lệch, soạn và KHÓA kế hoạch phân tích thống kê (SAP) + khung bảng kết quả (dummy tables). Dùng ở G1/G4. Cỡ mẫu/power do co-mau-nghien-cuu đảm nhiệm (G3) — agent này CHỈ tiêu thụ kết quả, không tự tính. Mọi việc sau khi đã xem dữ liệu thuộc về phan-tich-thong-ke. ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thiet-ke-nghien-cuu.md))
- **thin**: Fixes things. Use when asked to fix. ([BytesFromToby/Walkdown](https://github.com/BytesFromToby/Walkdown/blob/HEAD/Fixtures/02.6-builder/fit-repo/.claude/agents/thin.md) · ★4)
- **thin-content**: Separate short-but-useful pages from dead weight, and act on each differently. ([FMOps-ie/seo-agents](https://github.com/FMOps-ie/seo-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/33-thin-content.md))
- **thin-film-scientist**: Reasons from nucleation and growth modes, film stress, interfacial adhesion, and conformality across topography through spectroscopic ellipsometry, XRR, Stoney wafer-curvature, XPS/RBS, and standards like ASTM E2244 and ISO 9211 while treating columnar porosity, barrier pinholes, reactive-sputter hysteresis drift, and uncalibrated-QCM thickness error as first-class failure modes. - **thin-film-scientist**: Thin-Film Scientist: Reasons from nucleation and growth modes, film stress, interfacial adhesion, and conformality across topography through spectroscopic ellipsometry, XRR, Stoney wafer-curvature, XPS/RBS, and standards like ASTM E2244 and ISO 9211 while treating columnar porosity, barrier pinholes, reactive-sputter hysteresis drift, and uncalibrated-QCM thickness error as first-class failure modes. ([galeep/plugin-place](https://github.com/galeep/plugin-place/blob/HEAD/plugins/sci-agents-chemistry/agents/thin-film-scientist.md))
- **thin-persona-subagent-file-routing-to-a-knowledge-folder**: Each advisor in Nate Herk's council has a short subagent file that says how the person thinks, the one question they own and how they decide, then links to a folder of markdown files holding their rules, frameworks, positions, stories, voice and sources. ([model-bender/claudex-setup](https://github.com/model-bender/claudex-setup/blob/HEAD/lessons/agents/thin-persona-subagent-file-routing-to-a-knowledge-folder.md))
- **thingol**: Quality assurance - coverage auditing, semantic commits, changelog generation ([mscipio/bilt-transactions-export](https://github.com/mscipio/bilt-transactions-export/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/thingol.md))
- **think**: Deep-reasoning subagent for architecture decisions, complex debugging, and tradeoff analysis. Invoke when the user explicitly asks for deep analysis, or when a problem requires weighing multiple approaches, considering edge cases, or reasoning carefully about subtle bugs. ([dlants/magenta.nvim](https://github.com/dlants/magenta.nvim/blob/HEAD/node/server/src/agents/think.md) · ★466)
- **think**: Reasoning agent for decision analysis, scoring, debugging strategy, and debriefs. ([isaackehle/settings](https://github.com/isaackehle/settings/blob/HEAD/ai/config/agents/think.md) · ★1)
- **think**: Deep thinking agent for planning, strategy, analysis, and discussion ([bookwormsuf/sufyanskills](https://github.com/bookwormsuf/sufyanskills/blob/HEAD/sufyanskills/agents/think.md))
- **think**: Reasoning-heavy implementation agent for subtle Rust, daemon, concurrency, lifecycle, ownership, and recovery work that needs judgment beyond the default builder ([Mhirii/wkrun](https://github.com/Mhirii/wkrun/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/think.md))
- **think**: One deep-reasoning pass on the free Magistral seat (a TRUE thinking model on the big Mistral pool). Spawn 2–4 IN PARALLEL with distinct assigned angles for self-consistency on a hard question, as adversarial refuters in a root-cause hunt, or solo for an independent second derivation when the difficulty is pure reasoning and the scarce brain quota isn't warranted. Plentiful — fan out freely where the architect/reasoning seats would be rationed. Read-only; it reasons and reports, it does not edit. ([robsevo/serge-brain](https://github.com/robsevo/serge-brain/blob/HEAD/dot-serge/agents/think.md))
- **think-audit-reasoning**: Check whether a conclusion is sound before trusting it, by sorting evidence from inference, reconstructing the inferential climb, and reviewing through separated lenses. Runs the 3-skill think-audit-reasoning chain in its own context and returns only the finished reasoning audit, so the intermediate artifacts never enter the caller's conversation. Use when the caller wants the reasoning audit rather than the working. Do NOT use when the user wants to see or steer each step (run the /think-audit-reasoning command inline instead), or for a job one method answers (use that skill). ([product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills](https://github.com/product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/think-audit-reasoning.md) · ★26)
- **think-classifier**: Problem classification specialist who identifies problem type and characteristics for model selection. Use at start of /think:consider. ([rayk/lucid-toolkit](https://github.com/rayk/lucid-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/analyst/agents/think-classifier.md) · ★3)
- **think-clr-validator**: Adversarial logic validator using Goldratt's Categories of Legitimate Reservation. Validates causal links for TOC analyses. ([rayk/lucid-toolkit](https://github.com/rayk/lucid-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/analyst/agents/think-clr-validator.md) · ★3)
- **think-creator-converger**: Step 1 of the think-creator skill. Clusters an idea pool, scores each cluster on distinctiveness, value, and feasibility, protects sharp edges instead of averaging ideas into a safe compromise, picks ONE idea, and writes an explicit kill list for the rest. Use when a think-creator run needs its decision, or when asked to choose one idea from many. Chooses only — never builds, prototypes, or edits the idea. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-creator-converger.md))
- **think-creator-editor**: Step 3 of the think-creator skill. Takes the Prototyper's artifact and subtracts — cuts every element that doesn't carry the core idea, simplifies and sharpens what remains — then returns the edited artifact, a cut list with a reason for each cut, and a check that the core idea survived. Use when a think-creator run has a prototype to edit, or when asked to cut something to its essence. Subtracts only — never adds features or swaps the idea. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-creator-editor.md))
- **think-creator-prototyper**: Step 2 of the think-creator skill. Takes the one idea the Converger chose and makes the smallest tangible artifact that makes it real enough to react to — a one-page spec, text or HTML mock, landing-page copy, code sketch, or storyboard, chosen by medium — and returns it inline. Use when a think-creator run needs its idea made concrete, or when asked to prototype one idea. Makes only — never re-chooses the idea or writes production code. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-creator-prototyper.md))
- **think-explorer-expedition-planner**: Step 3 of the think-explorer skill. Takes the Frontier Scout's top regions and designs the cheapest probe for each — a falsifiable hypothesis, the smallest experiment, cost and time, and a go/no-go signal set in advance — ranked by learning per cost. Use when a think-explorer run has scouted white space and needs experiments, or when asked how to test a direction cheaply before committing. Plans probes only — never re-scouts the regions. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-explorer-expedition-planner.md))
- **think-explorer-frontier-scout**: Step 2 of the think-explorer skill. Reads the Territory Mapper's map, finds the empty regions and the adjacent possible, diagnoses why each one is empty — tried-and-failed, impossible, overlooked, or newly viable — and describes what could live in the open ones. Use when a think-explorer run has a landscape map and needs its white space, or when asked where the real gaps are. Scouts only — never designs the experiments. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-explorer-frontier-scout.md))
- **think-explorer-territory-mapper**: Step 1 of the think-explorer skill. Chooses the 2–3 dimensions that actually decide a challenge's outcome, places existing solutions, competitors, substitutes, and workarounds on them, and shows where everyone clusters. Use when a think-explorer run needs its landscape map, or when asked to map the options or players in a space. Maps only — never hunts for white space or proposes what to build. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-explorer-territory-mapper.md))
- **think-healer-pain-cartographer**: Step 1 of the think-healer skill. Maps who hurts and how much — every stakeholder including hidden ones, the job each is trying to get done, functional, emotional, and social pain scored by frequency × intensity, workarounds, and evidence — and separates stated wants from underlying needs. Use when a think-healer run needs its pain map, or when asked who is really hurting and how. Maps only — never diagnoses causes or proposes fixes. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-healer-pain-cartographer.md))
- **think-healer-remedy-designer**: Step 3 of the think-healer skill. Takes the diagnosed root causes and designs 3–5 humane remedies aimed at them, each with who it helps, how it relieves pain, a do-no-harm check on second-order effects and who could be hurt, and how you'd know it worked. Use when a think-healer run has a diagnosis and needs remedies, or when asked how to fix a pain at its cause without shifting it elsewhere. Treats diagnosed causes only — never re-diagnoses. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-healer-remedy-designer.md))
- **think-healer-root-cause-diagnostician**: Step 2 of the think-healer skill. Takes the Pain Cartographer's map, separates symptoms from causes with causal chains and 5 whys, surfaces systemic causes, and names the single highest-leverage root cause — flagging every link that rests on missing evidence. Use when a think-healer run has a pain map that needs diagnosing, or when asked for the root cause behind complaints, churn, or burnout. Diagnoses only — never designs a remedy. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-healer-root-cause-diagnostician.md))
- **think-imaginer-backcaster**: Step 3 of the think-imaginer skill, run in parallel with the Impossibility Auditor. Starts from the chosen vision and works backward — asking what had to be true just before this — until it reaches a first move startable this month, returning a dated chain of milestones. Use when a think-imaginer run has picked a vision and needs the path, or when asked to backcast from a future goal. Builds the path only — never judges feasibility. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-imaginer-backcaster.md))
- **think-imaginer-canvas-painter**: Step 1 of the think-imaginer skill. Paints 2–3 vivid end-state visions of a challenge as if the future already exists — present tense, concrete, sensory, a day in the life — with one deliberately extreme, ignoring today's constraints on purpose. Use when a think-imaginer run needs its visions, or when asked to picture a moonshot end state. Imagines only — never plans the path or judges feasibility. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-imaginer-canvas-painter.md))
- **think-imaginer-impossibility-auditor**: Step 3 of the think-imaginer skill, run in parallel with the Backcaster. Lists every leap between today and the chosen vision and classifies each as impossible (physics or logic), not-yet (waits on a tech or cost curve, dated), or unprecedented-but-possible (only social or organisational barriers). Use when a think-imaginer run needs its feasibility audit, or when asked if a moonshot is truly impossible. Audits only — never plans the path. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-imaginer-impossibility-auditor.md))
- **think-inspirer-audience-simulator**: Step 3 of the think-inspirer skill. Role-plays 3–4 audiences — the skeptic, the insider expert, the person it is for, and the decisive stakeholder — reading a draft line by line, and reports where each tuned out, disbelieved, or lit up, with revision notes flagged blocking or polish. Use when a think-inspirer run has a draft to test, or when asked how a pitch will land. Reacts and diagnoses only — never rewrites the piece. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-inspirer-audience-simulator.md))
- **think-inspirer-storyteller**: Step 2 of the think-inspirer skill. Turns the core truth into a story in the requested format — pitch, manifesto, keynote opener, memo, or launch post — following the arc world today, misfit belief, vision, proof, call to action, in concrete images and the idea's own voice. Use when a think-inspirer run needs its draft, or its one revision against audience notes. Writes only — never changes the core truth or grades its own draft. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-inspirer-storyteller.md))
- **think-inspirer-truth-distiller**: Step 1 of the think-inspirer skill. Distills an idea to its core truth — the one-sentence why written as a belief rather than a tagline, the enemy it fights, who it is for, the change in the world if it wins — plus the true proof points and native images a story can build on. Use when a think-inspirer run needs its foundation, or when asked to find the why behind an idea. Distills only — never writes the story or its copy. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-inspirer-truth-distiller.md))
- **think-inventor-decomposer**: Step 1 of the think-inventor skill. Breaks a challenge or its current solution into functions and attributes, lists the current value plus 3–5 alternatives for each as a morphological matrix, and names the core contradictions where improving one thing worsens another. Use when a think-inventor run needs its matrix, or when asked to take a problem apart for TRIZ or morphological analysis. Decomposes only — never proposes concepts. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-inventor-decomposer.md))
- **think-inventor-mechanism-sketcher**: Step 3 of the think-inventor skill. Turns the Recombiner's top 3 concepts into how-it-works specs — components, a numbered sequence of operation, the key insight, what must be built or proven, and the cheapest test that could kill it. Use when a think-inventor run has concepts that need to become mechanisms, or when asked how an idea would actually work. Specs mechanisms only — never invents new concepts or builds a prototype. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-inventor-mechanism-sketcher.md))
- **think-inventor-recombiner**: Step 2 of the think-inventor skill. Turns the Decomposer's matrix and contradictions into 8–12 non-obvious concepts, resolving contradictions with separation principles (time, space, condition, scale) and TRIZ-style inventive principles, with SCAMPER only as a supplement. Use when a think-inventor run has a matrix and needs concepts, or when asked to escape a trade-off without compromising. Generates concepts only — never writes specs. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-inventor-recombiner.md))
- **think-like-an-expert**: Looks at a problem from outside the task the work is busy with and returns every reframing and option it finds (the outcome behind the request, whose problem it is, what happens if nothing is done, cheaper or non-software ways, how other fields solve the same thing, whether the design taken as fixed really is), without discarding any, because choosing among them is the user's job. It explains how to think and what could be done instead, never code. Claude invokes it when the user asks to step back, gain altitude, go up a level or look from another angle ("da un paso atrás", "gana altitud", "s… ([javipazos/personal-claude-plugins](https://github.com/javipazos/personal-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/like-an-expert/agents/think-like-an-expert.md))
- **think-mcp-reporter**: Generates comprehensive eval reports with quality, semantic (LLM-as-Judge), accuracy, regression, and performance analysis for think-mcp tools. Use after think-mcp-tester completes. ([letsgomaslow/think](https://github.com/letsgomaslow/think/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-mcp-reporter.md))
- **think-mcp-tester**: Executes quality, regression, semantic, and performance evals for think-mcp tools. Use for ongoing validation and new mental model testing. ([letsgomaslow/think](https://github.com/letsgomaslow/think/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-mcp-tester.md))
- **think-misfit-consensus-mapper**: Step 1 of the think-misfit skill. Maps the insider orthodoxy around a challenge — how experts frame and solve it, the canonical solutions, the shared vocabulary, what the field treats as unthinkable, and its blind side — so the transplant step can measure distance from it. Use when a think-misfit run needs its consensus map, or when asked how insiders typically approach a problem. Maps only — never proposes analogies or alternatives. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-misfit-consensus-mapper.md))
- **think-misfit-domain-raider**: Step 2 of the think-misfit skill. Strips a challenge to its structural core with domain nouns removed, then raids 4–6 distant domains — nature, history, games, logistics, medicine, far-off industries — that already solved the same structure, and extracts each one's mechanism. Use when a think-misfit run needs its raw analogies, or when asked who far outside a field solved the same problem. Raids only — never adapts a mechanism to home. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-misfit-domain-raider.md))
- **think-misfit-transplanter**: Step 3 of the think-misfit skill. Takes the Domain Raider's mechanisms and the Consensus Mapper's orthodoxy, translates each mechanism concretely into the home domain, names the tissue rejection (what must adapt for it to take), and ranks the transplants by distance-from-orthodoxy × plausibility. Use when a think-misfit run has its raids and consensus map and needs the actual transplants. Transplants only — never raids new domains. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-misfit-transplanter.md))
- **think-orchestrator**: Model selection and coordination specialist who selects mental models and plans their parallel or sequential execution. ([rayk/lucid-toolkit](https://github.com/rayk/lucid-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/analyst/agents/think-orchestrator.md) · ★3)
- **think-rebel-fence-inspector**: Step 2 of the think-rebel skill. Puts each rule from the Rule Excavator on trial — reconstructs why the rule exists, checks whether that reason still holds today, and returns a verdict of load-bearing, weakened, or dead with evidence. Use when a think-rebel run has a rule inventory that needs judging, or when asked which constraints are real versus inherited. Judges only — never proposes how to break a rule. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-rebel-fence-inspector.md))
- **think-rebel-rule-breaker**: Step 3 of the think-rebel skill. Takes the rules the Fence Inspector ruled dead or weakened, inverts or deletes each one, and rebuilds the approach from first principles — returning concrete "without this rule, we could…" moves that leave load-bearing rules intact. Use when a think-rebel run has verdicts and needs the actual breaks, or when asked what a dropped constraint makes possible. Never re-litigates verdicts. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-rebel-rule-breaker.md))
- **think-rebel-rule-excavator**: Step 1 of the think-rebel skill. Digs up every explicit rule, unspoken assumption, inherited convention, self-imposed limit, and hard limit constraining a challenge, and returns them as a typed inventory for the next step to judge. Use when a think-rebel run needs its rule inventory, or when asked to list everything boxing in a problem. Discovers only — never judges or breaks a rule. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-rebel-rule-excavator.md))
- **think-research-framework**: Researches a named thinking method end to end and returns a verdict a human can act on - grades its evidence conservatively on the seven-tier model, proves or disproves distinctness against the shipped catalog, drafts its learning dossier, and emits a schema-valid proposed registry entry. Returns BUILD or FOLD, and folding is a success. Use when the question is whether a method deserves to ship, when an existing catalog judgment needs reproducing against current evidence, or when a family needs uncovered candidates shortlisted. Do NOT use to author a skill whose verdict is already settled, to… ([product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills](https://github.com/product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/think-research-framework.md) · ★26)
- **think-round-peg-frame-auditor**: Step 1 of the think-round-peg skill. Dissects a problem exactly as it was stated — the smuggled-in solution, whose problem it is, the success metric, timescale, scope boundary, unit, and the square hole it's forced into — runs an XY-problem check, and names the underlying need. Use when a think-round-peg run needs its frame audit, or when asked what a problem statement assumes. Audits only — never proposes a new framing. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-round-peg-frame-auditor.md))
- **think-round-peg-frame-judge**: Step 3 of the think-round-peg skill. Scores every framing from the Reframer, plus the original as a control, on leverage, fidelity to the real need, and actionability; rejects dodges; picks one primary and one wildcard; and writes the new problem statement and what the original frame excluded. Use when a think-round-peg run has candidate framings that need a verdict. Judges only — never solves the chosen problem. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-round-peg-frame-judge.md))
- **think-round-peg-reframer**: Step 2 of the think-round-peg skill. Turns the Frame Auditor's audit into 8–12 alternative "How might we…" framings, each built with a named lever such as zoom out, flip stakeholder, change metric, invert the goal, change the unit, or remove the embedded solution. Use when a think-round-peg run has its frame audit and needs reframes, or when asked for how-might-we questions on a problem. Generates only — never ranks or picks a framing. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-round-peg-reframer.md))
- **think-seer-anomaly-hunter**: Step 1 of the think-seer skill. One of three parallel lenses, it hunts the facts that contradict the dominant story — outliers, positive deviants, and cases that shouldn't work but do — reading any provided data first, and returns each with evidence, interpretation, and confidence kept apart. Use when a think-seer run needs its anomaly lens, or when asked what in the data doesn't fit. Observes only — never names insights or recommends. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-seer-anomaly-hunter.md))
- **think-seer-negative-space-observer**: Step 2 of the think-seer skill. One of three parallel lenses, it studies what is absent from a challenge — non-customers, unasked questions, silent failures, workarounds, the dog that didn't bark, and who isn't in the room — and returns each absence with where it looked, an interpretation, and confidence. Use when a think-seer run needs its absence lens, or when asked what nobody is talking about. Observes only — never names insights. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-seer-negative-space-observer.md))
- **think-seer-weak-signal-scout**: Step 3 of the think-seer skill. One of three parallel lenses, it scouts the edges for weak signals that the future is already here unevenly — fringe users, new tech, regulation, behaviour, and cost curves nearing a threshold — and rates each dated instance by strength × consequence. Use when a think-seer run needs its weak-signal lens, or when asked which early signs are being ignored. Observes only — never paints a vision or recommends. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-seer-weak-signal-scout.md))
- **think-stress-test-decision**: Pressure-test a consequential decision before committing, by comparing options, surfacing the conditions that must hold, imagining failure, and sanity-checking the estimate against base rates. Runs the 4-skill think-stress-test-decision chain in its own context and returns only the finished decision brief, so the intermediate artifacts never enter the caller's conversation. Do NOT use when the user wants to see or steer each step (run the /think-stress-test-decision command inline instead), or for a job one method answers (… ([product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills](https://github.com/product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/think-stress-test-decision.md) · ★26)

- **think-troublemaker-movement-miner**: Step 2 of the think-troublemaker skill. Applies lateral-thinking movement to each provocation — extract the principle, focus on the difference, simulate it moment to moment, find the positive, find where it would make sense — to mine practical ideas, dropping barren ones. Use when a think-troublemaker run has provocations to turn into usable ideas. Mines only — never invents provocations or attacks ideas. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-troublemaker-movement-miner.md))
- **think-troublemaker-provocateur**: Step 1 of the think-troublemaker skill. Generates 10–15 deliberately unreasonable provocations about a challenge using reversal, exaggeration, distortion, wishful thinking, escape, and random entry, each labelled with its technique, for the next step to mine. Use when a think-troublemaker run needs its provocations, or when asked for deliberately absurd starting points. Provokes only — never judges, ranks, or fixes a provocation. ([mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different](https://github.com/mohamadrezakoohkan/think-different/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/think-troublemaker-provocateur.md))
- **think-troublemaker-red-teamer**: Step 3 of the think-troublemaker skill. ([chrisallenlane/claude-swe-workflows](https://github.com/chrisallenlane/claude-swe-workflows/blob/HEAD/agents/thk-reframer.md) · ★21)
- **THK - Skeptic**: Good-faith skeptic that finds fatal flaws, serious concerns, and load-bearing assumptions in an idea or plan through an assigned critical lens. Used by /think-scrutinize alongside an advocate who rebuts its findings. ([chrisallenlane/claude-swe-workflows](https://github.com/chrisallenlane/claude-swe-workflows/blob/HEAD/agents/thk-skeptic.md) · ★21)
- **thm-check**: 定理の手順 5 段の検査役．本体のセッションが書いた導出を，結論の正しさを知らされない立場で 1 段ずつ読み，証明の穴と，仮定の表に無い仮定を探す．人工データで検算する台本を本体とは別に書いて流し，checks/&lt;E番号&gt;/thm-check.md に報告を書く．手順は docs/methods.md 10 節． ([snics6/MER-DA](https://github.com/snics6/MER-DA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thm-check.md) · 2 copies)
- **thm-claim**: 定理の手順 2 段の担当．割り当てられた 1 つの分野の見方（統計的学習理論，推定と識別，情報理論，信号処理とセンサーの融合のどれか）で，0 段の一覧の現象と 1 段の仮定の測定をもとに，MER-DA の手法を決める定理の主張を 2 個書く．証明はしない．ウェブ検索をせず，ほかの見方の担当の案を見ない．手順は docs/methods.md 10 節． ([snics6/MER-DA](https://github.com/snics6/MER-DA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thm-claim.md) · 2 copies)
- **thm-deep**: 定理の手順 3 段の 2 体目と 3 体目．ふるいで上位に残った定理の主張 1 個について，最も近い先行研究 2〜3 本の定理を原文で読み，仮定ごとに比べて，既知の定理への代入で済むかを判定する．手順は docs/methods.md 10 節． ([snics6/MER-DA](https://github.com/snics6/MER-DA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thm-deep.md) · 2 copies)
- **thm-open-problems**: 定理の手順 0 段 (b) の担当．MER-DA の設定に近い先行研究から，著者が自分で「未解決」「今後の課題」「扱っていない」と書いた問いを，原文の引用つきで一覧にし，後続で解かれたかを調べる．定理も手法も MER-DA の新しさも書かない．手順は docs/methods.md 10 節． ([snics6/MER-DA](https://github.com/snics6/MER-DA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thm-open-problems.md) · 2 copies)
- **thm-screen**: 定理の手順 3 段の 1 体目．2 段で出た 8 個の定理の主張を，複数の分野の語に言い換えた短い検索でふるい，各主張の新しさの種類と変わる判断を付けて，深く調べる上位 2 個を選ぶ．手順は docs/methods.md 10 節． ([snics6/MER-DA](https://github.com/snics6/MER-DA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thm-screen.md) · 2 copies)
- **thm-unexplained**: 定理の手順 0 段 (a) の担当．MER-DA の文書・ログ・結果ファイルを読み，今ある説明では説明できない実測を，数値と出所つきで一覧にする．定理も手法も新しさも書かない．手順は docs/methods.md 10 節． ([snics6/MER-DA](https://github.com/snics6/MER-DA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thm-unexplained.md) · 2 copies)
